16:41:23 RESULT: // UnmarshalJSON accepts an object or a list of objects; null leaves the list as it is.
func (l *OnboardingList) UnmarshalJSON(b []byte) error {
	t := bytes.TrimSpace(b)
	switch {
	case len(t) == 0 || string(t) == "null":
		return nil
	case t[0] == '[':
		var many []Onboarding
		if err := json.Unmarshal(t, &many); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		*l = many
	case t[0] == '{':
		var one Onboarding
		if err := json.Unmarshal(t, &one); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		*l = OnboardingList{one}
	default:
		return errors.New("must be an object or a list of objects")
	}
	return nil
}

// layout is the station-local text of every time in a fueling.
const layout = "2006-01-02T15:04:05"
func parseLocal(s string) (int64, error) {
	if !localShape.MatchString(s) {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("time %q is not YYYY-MM-DDThh:mm:ss", s)
	}
	t, err := time.ParseInLocation(layout, s, time.UTC)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("time %q: %v", s, err)
	}
	return t.Unix(), nil
}

func bndAdapterTestImport(fset *token.FileSet, f *ast.File, rel string, src []byte) []string {
	if strings.HasPrefix(rel, "internal/adapter/adaptertest/") {
